Conclusion: 磁场显著影响夸克物质的相结构，该模型能合理再现格点QCD的关键特征。

Abstract: We investigate the phase structure of magnetized quark matter within a nonlocal SU(2) Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model. Our work focuses on the interplay between temperature and imaginary chemical potential in the presence of an external uniform magnetic field, with emphasis on the deconfinement and Roberge-Weiss transitions. We analyze the dependence of the relevant critical temperatures on the external field, and we explore the sensitivity of the phase structure to different Polyakov loop effective potentials. Our results show qualitative agreement with lattice QCD findings, reproducing inverse magnetic catalysis in the chiral sector and a decreasing behavior of the Roberge-Weiss transition temperature with increasing magnetic field.

</details>


### [99] [Probing jet medium interaction with generalized projected energy correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01416)
*Balbeer Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了重离子碰撞中喷注的广义投影能量关联器（ν-关联器）在介质中的修正，发现介质诱导辐射对ν→0时的分布有调节作用，导致其相对于真空基线被参数化抑制，并在JEWEL模拟中得到验证；小角度分布随ν值变化显著，ν<1时大角度区域对介质修正更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重离子碰撞中喷注在致密介质中的演化行为，特别是通过ν-关联器探测介质对喷注结构的修改效应。

Method: 理论分析结合BDMPS-Z与半经典近似，辅以JEWEL模型的数值模拟。

Result: 介质调节了ν→0时的1/ν增强行为，使介质诱导辐射被抑制；小角度分布对ν敏感（ν<1与ν>1差异显著），而大角度增强在不同ν下定性相似；ν<1时小角度增强较弱，凸显大角度区域对介质修正更敏感。

Conclusion: ν-关联器（尤其ν<1）可作为探测喷注在致密介质中大角度辐射修正的更优观测量。

Abstract: We study medium modifications to generalized projected energy correlators, referred to as $ν$-correlators, measured on jets in heavy-ion collisions. While the vacuum distributions of these correlators exhibit a characteristic $1/ν$ enhancement, we show that in a dense medium this behavior is regulated within both the BDMPS-Z and semi-classical approximations as $ν\to 0$. As a result, contributions from medium-induced emissions become parametrically suppressed relative to the vacuum baseline. We observe a similar trend in the simulated events generated with JEWEL. We find that while the large angle enhancement is qualitatively similar across different values of $ν$, the small angle distribution changes drastically between $ν<1$ and $ν>1$. This indicates that small angle modifications, primarily driven by jet energy loss, are imprinted differently across $ν$-correlators. In particular, for $ν<1$ the small angle enhancement is less prominent which suggests that these correlators may provide improved sensitivity to medium modifications in the large angle region.

</details>


### [100] [Inelastic Dark Matter Signature at High Recoil Energy in LUX-ZEPLIN and CRESST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01475)
*Liangliang Su,Jin Min Yang,Wen-Na Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用内吸热暗物质模型解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的一个248 keV核反冲事件，指出需暗物质质量大于500 GeV、激发态与基态质量差约300 keV，并给出最佳拟合参数（1.105 TeV, 350 keV），该区域可由CRESST升级实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN合作组在2.84吨·年曝光量下观测到的单个高能（248 keV）核反冲事件，探索其是否可能源于内吸热暗物质（即暗物质需吸收能量才能散射）。

Method: 采用内吸热暗物质模型，同时考虑银河系晕中基态暗物质直接散射，以及地球内部上散射产生的激发态贡献；通过运动学分析和两区间似然分析确定参数空间。

Result: 解释该事件要求暗物质质量m_χ ≳ 500 GeV、质量劈裂δ ~ O(300) keV；当中子截面σ̄_n = 10⁻³⁷ cm²时，最佳拟合点为(m_χ, δ) ≈ (1.105 TeV, 350 keV)；且该δ值使地球内激发态产生在运动学上被禁止。

Conclusion: LUX-ZEPLIN的单事件异常可自洽地由内吸热暗物质模型解释，所推导出的参数区域具有可检验性，CRESST升级实验有望在未来验证该假设。

Abstract: The LUX--ZEPLIN Collaboration recently reported one event at $E_{\mathrm{nr}}=248\pm23~\mathrm{keV}$ with an exposure of $2.84~\mathrm{tonne\cdot yr}$. In this Letter, we interpret this feature using endothermic dark matter (DM). We consider both direct scattering of the surviving ground-state halo component and the contribution of excited states produced by terrestrial upscattering. Our calculations show that explaining the high-energy event requires $m_χ\gtrsim500~\mathrm{GeV}$ and a mass splitting of $\mathcal{O}(300)~\mathrm{keV}$, for which the production of excited states inside the Earth is kinematically forbidden. For $\barσ_n=10^{-37}~\mathrm{cm^2}$, an illustrative two-bin likelihood analysis yields a representative best-fit point at $(m_χ,δ)\simeq(1.105~\mathrm{TeV},350~\mathrm{keV})$. The preferred parameter region may be tested by the planned CRESST upgrade.

</details>


### [101] [Higgsino Above the Sea of Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01504)
*JiJi Fan,Matthew Reece*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了希格斯ino暗物质作为WIMP候选者的可行性，指出LZ实验观测到的单个核反冲事件可能由约1.1 TeV热产生希格斯ino（质量劈裂约350 keV）解释，并系统分析了其参数空间、紫外补全机制及非热宇宙学起源，并讨论了间接探测与未来对撞机等互补探测途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验最新报道的一个高能核反冲事件可能暗示暗物质信号，激发了对经典但仍有活力的希格斯ino暗物质模型的重新审视，尤其关注其是否能自然解释该事件及其对超对称理论结构的深层含义。

Method: 基于标准晕模型，结合LZ单事件统计，构建希格斯ino质量与质量劈裂二维参数空间；分析该劈裂对超对称谱（如电弱微子、重Higgs标量）能标的压制效应；探讨实现轻希格斯ino与重其余超伴子共存的UV补全机制；并简要评估非热产生（如gravitino/模场衰变）及多信使探测（间接探测、对撞机）的可行性。

Result: 确定了可解释LZ单事件的希格斯ino质量（~1.1 TeV）与质量劈裂（~350 keV）组合；推导出电弱微子与重Higgs标量需处于10³–10⁴ TeV能标；提出若干UV机制以实现分层超对称谱；指出非热起源与多信使探测具有可观测性。

Conclusion: LZ单事件虽统计显著性低，却为希格斯ino暗物质提供了迄今最具体的实验线索；该场景要求超对称呈现明显谱分层，挑战传统统一尺度假设，同时推动对暗物质速度分布高能尾、非热宇宙学及下一代实验的深入研究。

Abstract: Higgsino dark matter is a classic, but still viable, weakly-interacting massive particle (WIMP) scenario that could point to supersymmetry, a new spacetime symmetry of nature. It is a simple realization of the "inelastic dark matter" mechanism, where its scattering off a nucleus occurs between two nearly degenerate states. Intriguingly, very recently the LZ collaboration reported a single nuclear recoil event at a high recoil energy. Assuming that this event is a dark matter candidate, it could be explained by higgsino dark matter, e.g., a 1.1 TeV thermal higgsino with a mass splitting of about 350 keV (using the standard halo model; a much better understanding of the high speed tail in the dark matter velocity distribution is needed for a more precise determination). We map out the parameter space in the higgsino mass and splitting plane that could account for this single LZ event. The needed mass splitting implies that the electroweak gauginos and heavy Higgs scalars in supersymmetry have to be at a much higher scale $\sim (10^3\text{ to }10^4)$ TeV. We discuss possible UV completions to generate a SUSY spectrum in which all superpartners are at a common scale, except for a much lighter higgsino mass $μ$. Besides the thermal benchmark, we comment on possible non-thermal cosmological scenarios of higgsino dark matter with gravitino and modulus decays. The higgsino dark matter scenario could also be searched for at other complementary probes such as indirect detection and future colliders. One striking LZ event may herald an exciting future of further experimental developments.

</details>


### [102] [TeV Higgsino Dark Matter from LZ Nuclear Recoil to Fermi-LAT Gamma Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01590)
*Lei Wu,Yang Zhang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出1.1 TeV的Higgsino暗物质可同时解释LZ实验的核反冲超出和Fermi-LAT银河系中心数据的轻微偏好，并受IceCube太阳中微子限制，预测伽马射线谱线信号可在CTAO未来探测中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgsino暗物质是MSSM中最简且典型的WIMP候选者，但其能否同时解释多个实验异常并满足现有约束尚待研究。

Method: 结合LZ核反冲数据、Fermi-LAT银河系中心14年伽马数据、IceCube 10年太阳中微子限制，通过 relic abundance 和 recoil spectrum 拟合确定Higgsino质量与质量劈裂，并预测伽马射线信号可观测性。

Result: 确定Higgsino质量约1.1 TeV、质量劈裂为数百keV；获得与多实验兼容的参数空间；预测伽马射线谱线和端点信号接近H.E.S.S.灵敏度、在CTAO覆盖范围内。

Conclusion: 1.1 TeV Higgsino暗物质是一个自洽、可检验的经典WIMP候选者，近期伽马射线观测有望对其做出决定性检验。

Abstract: The Higgsino dark matter of the minimal supersymmetric standard model is one of the most minimal and canonical WIMP candidates. We demonstrate that this classic candidate can account for both the high-energy nuclear-recoil excess reported by the LZ experiment and the mild preference in the 14 years of FermiLAT Galactic-center data, with the relic abundance fixing the Higgsino dark matter mass near 1.1 TeV and the recoil spectrum selecting a mass splitting of a few hundred keV. Together with the 10-year IceCube solar-neutrino constraint, the Higgsino interpretation further pins down a viable region of parameter space. It predicts a Galactic-center gamma-ray line and endpoint signal near the present H.E.S.S. sensitivity and within CTAO's projected reach, making this classic candidate testable in the near future.

</details>


### [103] [Fermionic Dark Matter Absorption and the High-Energy Event in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01592)
*Yuanchao Lou,hih-Ting Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验在248±32.5 keVnr能区观测到一个事例，本文提出中性流费米子暗物质被氙核吸收可自然解释该事例，但该解释与KamLAND中子发射道数据存在显著张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验在高能核反冲窗口观测到的单个候选事例，同时保持低能谱与背景预期一致。

Method: 构建中性流费米子暗物质在氙核上的相干与非相干吸收模型，结合有效场论框架，拟合事例能量与截面，并通过KamLAND对碳核中子发射道的再分析施加约束。

Result: 发现质量约247 MeV的暗物质通过单核子吸收可产生~248 keVnr单能核反冲，所需吸收截面为1.07×10⁻⁴⁶ cm²（对应能标Λ≈11.5 TeV），但该参数空间被KamLAND数据排除。

Conclusion: LZ事例的暗物质吸收解释虽在LZ数据内自洽，却与KamLAND结果存在显著张力，需未来高能专项分析进一步检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single candidate event in the high-energy nuclear recoil window $248\pm32.5\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$ with an exposure of $2.80\ \mathrm{ton}\cdot\mathrm{yr}$, while the low-energy spectrum remains consistent with background expectations. We demonstrate that this excess can be naturally explained by the neutral-current absorption of fermionic dark matter on xenon nuclei. For a dark matter mass $m_χ\simeq 247\ \mathrm{MeV}$, the coherent absorption process produces a monoenergetic nuclear recoil at $E_R \simeq 248\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$. At this momentum transfer, the absorption process enters the incoherent regime, where scattering off individual nucleons produces a broad recoil spectrum extending from $\sim 200\ \mathrm{keV}$ to $100.2\ \mathrm{MeV}$. We show that a single effective field theory coupling can simultaneously produce one event in the $248\pm32.5\ \mathrm{keV}_{\mathrm{nr}}$ window while remaining consistent with the non-observation of events in neighboring energy regions. The required single-nucleon absorption cross section is $σ_{χN}^{\mathrm{NC}} = 1.07\times10^{-46}\ \mathrm{cm}^2$, corresponding to an effective field theory scale $Λ\simeq 11.5\ \mathrm{TeV}$. However, a recasting analysis of KamLAND data on the neutron-emission channel $χ+{}^{12}\mathrm{C} \to ν+ n + {}^{11}\mathrm{C}^*$ excludes this benchmark parameter space, establishing a significant tension between the LZ excess interpretation and existing constraints from large-volume scintillator detectors. We discuss the implications of this tension and prospects for resolving it with future dedicated high-energy analyses.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Quantum corrections to the black hole entropy using the brick wall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00096)
*Gopinath Guin,Arpita Jana,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了砖墙模型在几种黑洞（Reissner-Nordström、量子修正Schwarzschild及量子修正Reissner-Nordström）中的应用，发现该模型可给出熵的对数修正项，且未采用近视界近似，所得修正熵大于Bekenstein-Hawking熵，与Barrow熵结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为避免探针场自由能或熵的发散，需引入红外（IR）截断（即砖墙模型），并探究其在不同黑洞背景下的熵修正效果。

Method: 将砖墙模型应用于Reissner-Nordström、量子修正Schwarzschild和量子修正Reissner-Nordström黑洞，不采用近视界近似处理度规函数，从而获得更精确的熵修正。

Result: 得到黑洞性熵的对数修正项（次主导阶），且修正后的熵大于标准Bekenstein-Hawking熵。

Conclusion: 砖墙模型在多种黑洞背景下可自然导出对数熵修正，且不依赖近视界近似，其结果支持Barrow熵等更大熵值的理论框架。

Abstract: It was suggested by 't Hooft that to avoid the divergence of the free energy or entropy of the probe fields, one has to introduce an infrared (IR) cutoff. This introduction of the IR cutoff, namely, the brick wall model, has been studied for the Schwarzschild black hole and has been shown by 't Hooft that to have the entropy of the order of Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, the cutoff must be of the order of Planck length. In this study, we investigated the brick wall model in the context of a Reissner-Nordstróm, quantum corrected Schwarzschild and quantum corrected Reissner-Nordström black holes. In particular, we have shown that the brick wall model can give logarithmic correction to the black hole entropy in the subleading order. The important aspect of our analysis is that we have not considered the near horizon approximation of the lapse function and get more accurate correction to the entropy. This corrected entropy has an important feature that it is larger than the usual Bekenstein-Hawking entropy which aligns with the result of Barrow entropy.

</details>


### [105] [Accretion, mergers, and metastability of fuzzy spheres in a three-matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00328)
*M. Hrmo,S. Kováčik,K. Magdolenová,A. Manta,K. Nedeľková,P. Rusnák,H. C. Steinacker,J. Tekel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含多种模糊球解的三矩阵模型，重点探讨了初始配置下热力学优选态的演化速度及是否存在长寿命亚稳态构型。研究发现，在大矩阵极限下，答案取决于初始配置的离散度；基本跃迁过程为‘吸积’，即一个球吸收另一个球的单个单元，该过程由球心涨落驱动；通过长时间模拟测量这些涨落，结果与单圈有效作用量定量一致；对于多球态，内层球的刚度随周围层数对数增长，导致类似洋葱结构的同心构型（描述涌现的三维量子空间）寿命显著延长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在三矩阵模型中，从给定初始构型出发，系统是否能快速达到热力学优选态，还是存在支持有趣物理现象的长寿命亚稳态。

Method: 通过数值模拟测量球心涨落，分析吸积型基本跃迁过程，并结合单圈有效作用量理论进行对比；研究多球态中内层球刚度随层数的变化规律。

Result: 发现大矩阵极限下演化行为依赖于初始配置离散度；吸积过程由球心涨落驱动，且模拟结果与单圈有效作用量定量吻合；同心洋葱状多球构型因内层球刚度对数增长而具有显著延长的寿命。

Conclusion: 该模型中存在丰富的长寿命亚稳态（如洋葱状构型），可支撑涌现三维量子空间等新奇物理，且其动力学由球心涨落主导，理论与模拟高度一致。

Abstract: A three-matrix model that contains a plethora of fuzzy sphere solutions was defined twenty years ago. It has been shown that in a certain regime of the model, combinations of fuzzy spheres become classical solutions of the model. In the original paper and subsequent works, the stability of specific states has been discussed, showing that only a single largest fuzzy-sphere configuration is stable. Here, we focus on a complementary question: starting from some initial configuration, is the thermodynamically preferred state approached rapidly, or are there other long-lived, metastable configurations which may support interesting physics? We find that the answer in the large-matrix limit depends on the dispersion of the initial configuration. We identify the elementary transition as an accretion, in which one sphere absorbs a single unit from another, and show that it is driven by fluctuations of the sphere centers. By measuring these fluctuations in long simulations, we find quantitative agreement with the one-loop effective action, with no free parameters. For multi-sphere states, we find that the stiffness of an inner sphere grows logarithmically with the number of surrounding layers, so that concentric onion-like configurations -- describing an emergent three-dimensional quantum space -- become increasingly long-lived.

</details>


### [106] [Black Hole-Tower Correspondence: Species backreaction in minimal black hole limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00119)
*Miquel Aparici,Alvaro Herráez,Joaquin Masias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了黑洞-弦对应关系，提出了黑洞-塔对应关系，并通过热力学分析、引力反作用及世界面拓扑分析，验证了其在自相互作用下的鲁棒性，并揭示了II型弦论中相变受拓扑障碍限制，需借助非微扰过程实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展黑洞-弦对应到更一般的黑洞-塔对应，特别是在Kaluza-Klein塔情形下，探究其热力学、引力反作用及相变连续性。

Method: 结合自由热力学分析、一阶引力反作用计算、线性sigma模型与拓扑（配边理论、上同调）分析，比较异质弦与II型弦中的相变行为。

Result: 证实Kaluza-Klein塔在自相互作用下仍满足S∼N_sp的对应；异质弦中存在光滑插值，而II型弦因配边群导致的荷格差异产生微扰障碍，相变需非微扰机制。

Conclusion: 黑洞-塔对应具有鲁棒性；II型弦中相变受拓扑障碍限制，支持协边猜想关于非微扰电荷破坏过程的预言。

Abstract: The Black Hole-String Correspondence identifies the microstates of a minimal black hole with those of a highly excited string. In decompactification limits, where the light states are Kaluza--Klein modes, its proposed counterpart is the Black Hole-Tower Correspondence at the species scale. We extend it in three directions. First, we expand the free thermodynamic analysis to include towers with string-oscillators and Kaluza--Klein modes, and clarify whether the ensemble connects to a $d$-dimensional black hole or a wrapped black string. Second, we include gravitational backreaction in the pure Kaluza--Klein tower transition. With no winding-tachyon analogue, the interpolating configuration is the one-loop Kaluza--Klein gas, which reorganizes into higher-dimensional radiation and yields self-gravitating solutions whose instability points to the black string and whose thermodynamics match it at the correspondence point, $S\sim N_{\rm sp}$, where the size approaches the species length. This shows that the matching is robust under self-interactions. Third, we ask whether the two phases are continuously connected, revisiting and extending the worldsheet and topological analyses of the Black Hole-String case to include also Kaluza--Klein towers. Linear sigma model families interpolate smoothly in the heterotic string, while in type II the transition is obstructed. The cobordism classes of the two saddles agree in every structure we check, but their brane-charge lattices, given by bordism groups, differ, which is the source of the type II obstruction. This obstruction is thus perturbative, showing that the transition can only proceed through the non-perturbative, charge-violating processes of the kind predicted by the Cobordism Conjecture.

</details>


### [107] [Subleading Collinear Limits of Yang--Mills Amplitudes from Recursions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00120)
*Jin Dong,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了树级色序Yang-Mills振幅的次领头邻近共线极限，提出了基于Lorentz零旋转和改进递归方法（Berends-Giele与BCFW）的计算框架，并推广至四维及一般维度，同时关联到Einstein-Yang-Mills振幅与弦论高阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Yang-Mills振幅在共线极限下的精细结构，特别是次领头项，对散射振幅解析性质、软/共线因子化及弦理论与场论对应具有重要意义。

Method: 利用Lorentz null旋转保持极化横性与规范等价性；引入通道删除的Berends-Giele递归计算硬贡献；在四维中构建共线感知的BCFW递归，以MHV/anti-MHV为终端；结合open-closed disk关系导出EYM振幅及其弦修正。

Result: 得到任意维度下次领头邻近共线系数的分离表达式；四维中给出全解析递归构造；实现极化任意组合与动量分数任意分割下的结果；导出一引力子EYM振幅及高导数弦修正；提供配套Mathematica实现。

Conclusion: 次领头共线结构可系统递归构造，兼具普适性与计算实用性，为振幅因子化、弦-场论映射及数值工具开发提供了新路径。

Abstract: We study the subleading adjacent-collinear limit of tree-level color-ordered Yang--Mills amplitudes. In general dimensions, a Lorentz null rotation transports the polarizations of collinear legs along the collinear path while preserving transversality and gauge equivalence. The finite coefficient then separates into a pole-free hard contribution, computed by a channel-deleted Berends--Giele recursion, and an explicit contribution from the adjacent factorization channel. In four dimensions, we construct the same coefficient directly from a collinear-aware Britto-Cachazo-Feng-Witten recursion whose terminal data are closed MHV and $\overline{\rm MHV}$ formulas. Both constructions apply at arbitrary momentum fraction and extend to collinear gluons with distinct polarizations; at the symmetric split, the auxiliary-vector dependence cancels for equal polarizations and for the polarization-symmetrized mixed limit. Open--closed disk relations then give a direct construction of one-graviton Einstein--Yang--Mills amplitudes and their higher-derivative string corrections from the recursively generated coefficients. A self-contained \textsc{Mathematica} implementation accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [108] [Classification of Rational $c=1$ Vertex Operator Algebras and Vertex Operator Superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00122)
*Terry Gannon,Brandon C. Rayhaun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对c=1的顶点算子代数（VOA）和超代数（VOSA）进行了严格的数学分类，证明其必为秩1整格L对应的格VO(S)A V_L，或其有限群G的轨道构造V_L^G；关键工具是仅依赖真空特征的判据，用于判断整共形维数单模是否生成与Rep(G)辫子等价的对称融合子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理上，旨在分类玻色/费米有理c=1共形场论（CFT）全息扇区可能的弦代数（即手征代数），要求非恒等原初场具有正共形维数；数学上，填补c=1 VO(S)A严格分类的空白，并建立手征代数与3维拓扑量子场论边界条件的联系。

Method: 基于真空字符建立一般性判据，判定整共形维数单模生成的融合范畴是否辫子等价于某有限群G的表示范畴Rep(G)；结合已知c=1格VO(S)A及其自同构群G的结构，完成枚举分类；将手征代数解释为U(1)_k陈-西蒙斯理论的全息边界。

Result: 完全枚举了‘良好’的c=1 VO(S)A：全部为V_L或V_L^G形式；明确了对应3维TQFT中手征代数作为U(1)_k理论边界的物理图像；揭示了这些代数的非可逆对称性结构。

Conclusion: 任何满足条件的c=1 VO(S)A均可由秩1整格及其有限群轨道构造唯一确定；该分类为后续完整c=1 CFT（含非手征部分）的分类奠定基础，并统一了数学VO(S)A理论与物理CFT、TQFT的框架。

Abstract: For mathematicians: In this first in a series of two papers, we give a mathematically rigorous classification of (sufficiently nice) $c=1$ vertex operator algebras (VOAs) and vertex operator superalgebras (VOSAs). We confirm the lore that any such VO(S)A is either a lattice VO(S)A $V_L$ associated to a rank-1 integral lattice $L$, or can be obtained as an orbifold thereof, i.e. a $G$-invariant subalgebra $V_L^G$ for some finite group $G$ of automorphisms. All such $G$ are known, allowing for an explicit enumeration of nice $c=1$ VO(S)As. A key ingredient in our approach is to establish a general criterion for nice VOAs, requiring knowledge only of the vacuum character, for testing when the simple modules with integer conformal dimension span a symmetric fusion subcategory which is braided tensor equivalent to $Rep(G)$. In our companion paper, we calculate the ribbon auto-equivalences of the representation categories of the nice $c=1$ VOAs, and leverage this to obtain the classification of nice bosonic and fermionic $c=1$ full conformal field theories.
  For physicists: We rigorously classify the chiral algebras that can arise in the holomorphic sector of a bosonic or fermionic rational $c=1$ conformal field theory (CFT) whose non-identity primaries all have positive conformal dimension. Thinking of chiral algebras as gapless boundary conditions of 3D topological quantum field theories (TQFTs), our result says that any such chiral algebra is either a holomorphic boundary of $U(1)_k$ Chern-Simons theory, or can be obtained by passing to the $G$-invariant states thereof for some finite group $G$ of symmetries. We explicitly enumerate these chiral algebras and also discuss their non-invertible symmetries. In a companion paper, we build on these results using techniques from the study of 3D TQFTs to classify full bosonic and fermionic rational $c=1$ CFTs.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Complexity Dynamics for Disjoint Subsystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00126)
*Yale Fan,Nicholas Hunter-Jones,Andreas Karch,Minju Kum,Shivan Mittal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非连通子系统（由多个不相交区域组成）的量子复杂度演化，发现其相比连通子系统展现出新奇物理现象：热平衡下小尺寸子系统可具有高复杂度而其大补集却保持低复杂度；且将子系统分割为m个部分可使复杂度达到平衡的时间缩短m倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂度是探测混沌、热化和黑洞内部的重要工具，但此前研究几乎仅限于单连通子系统，本文旨在探索非连通子系统的复杂度行为及其新物理内涵。

Method: 结合全息对偶（holography）与随机量子电路（random quantum circuits）两种互补方法进行理论分析与建模。

Result: 1）有限温度下，占据系统不到一半的非连通子系统可在晚期携带高复杂度，而其较大补集复杂度仍低，该‘倒置层级’现象在无限温极限（即随机电路模型）下消失；2）将已早达平衡的子系统分割为m个不相交部分，可使平衡时间加速m倍。

Conclusion: 非连通子系统的复杂度演化揭示了热效应与几何-计算复杂度对应的新维度，推动在量子动力学中探索更丰富的复杂度现象。

Abstract: Quantum complexity has emerged as a natural probe of chaos, thermalization, and the black hole interior on timescales long after local observables have equilibrated. However, its time evolution has been studied almost exclusively in subsystems confined to a single connected region. We show, using complementary tools from holography and random quantum circuits, that noncontiguous subsystems composed of multiple disjoint regions give rise to qualitatively new physics compared to the contiguous case. First, at finite temperature, a subsystem occupying less than half of the total system can carry high complexity at late times, even as the complexity of its larger complement remains low. This inversion of the usual hierarchy is intrinsically thermal: it vanishes in the infinite-temperature limit, which is the regime modeled by random quantum circuits. Second, if a subsystem's complexity equilibrates at an early time, then fragmenting it into $m$ disjoint components can further reduce this timescale by a factor of $m$, a phenomenon we exhibit in both holography and random quantum circuits. These results not only sharpen the correspondence between geometric and computational notions of complexity, but also motivate the search for novel complexity phenomena in quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [A 1/4-BPS chiral algebra in D1D5: from charge concentration to BPS chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00131)
*Alexandre Belin,Suzanne Bintanja,Alessandra Gnecchi,Damian R. Musk,Kyriakos Papadodimas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究D1D5 CFT中1/4-BPS算符的手征代数及有理关联函数，利用交叉对称性给出了一族远离电荷聚集位置的1/4-BPS态存在的非微扰证明，并探讨其对BPS混沌的意义及在轨道点处计算了晚期BPS平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决D1D5 CFT中BPS态的电荷聚集难题，该难题类似于但比N=4 SYM更简单。

Method: 基于超荷上同调构建1/4-BPS算符的手征代数，结合交叉对称性和有理关联函数进行分析。

Result: 非微扰地证明了任意耦合和有限中心荷下存在一族远离电荷聚集位置的1/4-BPS态；计算了轨道点处的晚期BPS平台；讨论了BPS混沌的可能含义。

Conclusion: D1D5 CFT中存在稳定的、非局域于电荷聚集流形的1/4-BPS态，这为理解其非微扰结构和BPS混沌提供了新视角。

Abstract: The D1D5 CFT exhibits a charge concentration puzzle for BPS states, similar to, but simpler than, that of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. We consider the chiral algebra of 1/4-BPS operators in the D1D5 CFT arising from a supercharge cohomology, and the resulting rational correlators. Using crossing symmetry and this chiral algebra, we establish a non-perturbative proof of the existence of a family of 1/4-BPS states away from the charge concentration locus for general coupling and finite central charge. We further discuss possible implications for BPS chaos in the D1D5 CFT and compute a late-time BPS plateau at the orbifold point.

</details>


### [111] [Regulating Free-scalar and Yang--Lee 5d CFTs on the Fuzzy Four Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00148)
*Jiangyuan Qian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了5维自由标量CFT和Yang-Lee CFT在模糊4球上的非对易球正则化模型，观测到连续相变和共形塔，并通过ED和DMRG方法计算了关键标量算子的反常维数，结果与ε展开和Gliozzi截断自举法一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非对易球正则化方法从3维、4维CFT拓展至5维CFT，以更精确地计算高维共形数据并验证其普适性。

Method: 在模糊4球上构造5维自由标量CFT和Yang-Lee CFT模型；使用精确对角化（ED）和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法计算标量原初算子的反常维数。

Result: 观测到连续相变和共形塔结构；获得5维自由标量CFT前三个标量原初算子；计算得到5维Yang-Lee CFT中Δ_φ和Δ_{φ³}，与ε展开和Gliozzi截断自举结果具有良好一致性。

Conclusion: 非对易球正则化方法可成功推广至5维CFT，为高维共形场论的数值研究提供了有效新工具，并支持该方法在不同维度下的普适适用性。

Abstract: Non-commutative sphere regularization of CFT has become a powerful tool for computing conformal data of 3d CFTs and was recently extended to 4d CFTs. In this work we further construct 5d free-scalar CFT and Yang-Lee CFT on a fuzzy 4-sphere. We observe a continuous phase transition and conformal towers in these examples. We obtain the first three scalar primaries of the 5d free-scalar CFT, furthermore, we compute the 5d Yang-Lee $Δ_φ,Δ_{φ^3}$ with $N_e=10$ fermions using exact diagonalization (ED) and $N_e=20$ fermions using density matrix renormalization group (DMRG), obtaining relatively good agreement with both $ε$-expansion and the Gliozzi truncated bootstrap result.

</details>


### [112] [On the mass spectrum of Viel-dreibein gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00150)
*Eloy Ayón-Beato,Elizabeth Rodríguez Querts*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Viel-dreibein引力（一种三维N重自旋-2场相互作用理论）中的AdS波构型，通过将系统重写为单dreibein的高阶导数理论，并利用A-超几何函数对运动方程进行因式分解，从而独立于引力数目N地确定了质量谱；进一步识别出包括BF界在内的所有临界点，发现多种对数模及log^{χ−1}渐近行为（χ≤N），暗示其可能对偶于对数共形场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Viel-dreibein引力这一多引力理论的质量谱，并构造其AdS波解，进而探索其对偶共形场论特性。

Method: 将N重dreibein系统约化为单dreibein的2N阶高导理论；利用A-超几何函数表示多项式根，实现运动方程分解为N个Klein-Gordon算子的乘积。

Result: 独立于N地解析得到完整质量谱；系统性找出所有临界点（含超越BF界的退化点）；发现χ≤N类log^{χ−1}渐近行为的新对数模。

Conclusion: 该方法为多引力理论的质量分析与AdS/CFT中对数CFT对偶研究提供了普适工具，揭示了多重引力间丰富的临界结构与非单位模行为。

Abstract: We study the AdS-wave configurations of Viel-dreibein gravity, a three-dimensional theory of $N$ interacting spin-2 fields, as a pretext to characterize the mass spectrum of this multigravity theory. We manage to implement the known strategy of rewriting the system as a higher-derivative theory for a single dreibein, thereby deriving the precise $2N$th-order equation governing the dynamics of the AdS-wave profile in question. The second novelty we introduce is a factorization of such an equation as the product of $N$ Klein-Gordon operators, exploiting the recent representation of the roots of any $N$th-degree polynomial in terms of the increasingly celebrated $\mathscr{A}$-hypergeometric functions. Remarkably, this allows us to determine the mass spectrum of the full theory independently of the number of involved gravities, which is essential for an explicit construction of the AdS-wave solutions. This enables us to explore the parameter space of the theory and find all its critical points (not only the Breitenlohner-Freedman bounds) where a much wider range of degeneracies in the squared masses arises. At those points, the solutions allow a zoo of new logarithmic modes leading to several $\log^{χ-1}$ asymptotic behaviors, with $χ\le N$, a signature of the existence of allegedly dual logarithmic conformal field theories.

</details>


### [113] [Subleading Collinear Limits of Yang-Mills Amplitudes from Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00153)
*Jin Dong,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了杨-米尔斯振幅的次主导共线扇区由爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯（EYM）振幅及其高阶导数修正所控制，并通过开闭弦盘振幅提取完整次主导共线极限，利用无符号第一类斯特林数对结果进行系统分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解杨-米尔斯振幅在共线极限下的结构，特别是次主导项的起源与组织方式，以深化对规范/引力对偶及弦论低能有效理论的认识。

Method: 利用开闭弦盘振幅提取等螺旋度共线对的严格次主导共线极限；推导含非线性运动学系数的BCJ型关系；结合EYM振幅与高阶导数修正构建简化基；运用无符号第一类斯特林数对各项进行分类。

Result: 发现n点次主导共线数据可约化为维数为½(n−3)! 的基，该基由(n−4)!个独立EYM子振幅和½(n−5)(n−4)!个含一个引力子与n−2个胶子的高阶导数修正组成；所有成分按无符号第一类斯特林数 [n−3 atop k] 分层。

Conclusion: 杨-米尔斯振幅的次主导共线结构完全由EYM理论及其高阶导数修正刻画，且其组合规律由第一类斯特林数精确编码，揭示了共线极限中规范场与引力场深层统一性。

Abstract: We show that the subleading collinear sector of Yang-Mills (YM) amplitudes is controlled by ordinary Einstein-Yang-Mills (EYM) amplitudes and their higher-derivative corrections. The complete set of strict subleading collinear limits associated with an equal-helicity collinear pair can be extracted from open-closed string disk amplitudes. The latter generate $\frac12 (n-3)!$ BCJ-like relations with non-linear kinematic coefficients, reducing the $(n-3)!$ collinear data to a basis of dimension $\frac12 (n-3)!$. This reduced basis can be represented by gravitational amplitudes in EYM theories and by their higher-derivative open-string corrections. At multiplicity $n$ this gravitational basis consists of the $(n-4)!$ independent EYM subamplitudes together with $\frac12 (n-5)(n-4)!$ higher-order corrections involving one graviton and $n-2$ gluons. The resulting decomposition is governed by unsigned Stirling numbers of the first kind: the ordinary EYM amplitudes correspond to the sector $\left[{n-3\atop 1}\right]$, the higher-order BCJ-like relations to even sectors $\left[{n-3\atop 2j}\right]$, and the higher-derivative EYM corrections to odd sectors $\left[{n-3\atop 2j+1}\right]$ with $j\geq 1$.

</details>


### [114] [Exploring thermal order in conformal theories with multiple scalars coupled to an $O(N)$ vector field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00160)
*Soumyadeep Chaudhuri,Bilal Hawashin,Eliezer Rabinovici,Michael M. Scherer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在d=4-ε和d=3维共形场论（CFT）中，多个标量场与O(N)矢量场耦合时的热序行为；发现在(4-ε)维中存在两个Wilson-Fisher型不动点，并揭示了Z₂^M ⋊ S_M对称性在不同M和N下的自发破缺行为；通过函数重整化群方法分析其向三维的延拓，发现不动点在到达d=3前即进入复平面；而在三维大N极限下，模型展现出共形流形结构，并证明在一定参数区域内，所有M个标量在任意非零温度下均获得热期望值，导致符号翻转对称性在所有非零温度下自发破缺，提供了一类具有丰富持久热对称破缺模式的新CFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形场论中热序与对称性破缺行为，特别是多标量场与O(N)矢量场耦合系统在不同维度下的临界行为与相变特性。

Method: 采用epsilon展开、函数重整化群（FRG）方法及大N展开（1/N展开）分析RG流与不动点结构，并结合对称性分析与共形流形构造。

Result: 在(4-ε)维中发现依赖于M和N的双不动点窗口；Z₂^M ⋊ S_M对称性在M=2且N足够大时于任意非零温度下自发破缺，而M>2时不破缺；这些不动点在FRG流向d=3过程中消失于复平面；在三维大N下，模型存在O(1/N)精度的共形流形，且在其子空间中可实现所有M个标量在任意T>0下获得热期望值，导致符号翻转对称性持久破缺。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一类新型大N共形场论，其在任意非零温度下表现出稳定的对称性自发破缺，拓展了热CFT中有序相的理解，并表明高维epsilon展开结果未必能光滑延拓至三维物理情形。

Abstract: We study thermal order in conformal field theories (CFTs) in $d=4-ε$ and $d=3$ dimensions where several scalars are coupled to an $O(N)$ vector field. In $(4-ε)$ dimensions, we consider models coupling a cubic model of $M$ scalars with $\mathbb{Z}_2^M\rtimes S_M$ symmetry (corresponding to sign flips and permutations of the scalars) to an $O(N)$ vector model. For any $M$, we find a window of $N$ in which two Wilson-Fisher-like fixed points exist. We show that the $\mathbb{Z}_2^M\rtimes S_M$ symmetry is spontaneously broken at arbitrary nonzero temperatures for $M=2$ and sufficiently large $N$ within this window, but it remains unbroken for $M>2$. Using the functional renormalisation group to continue these fixed points towards three dimensions, we find that they collide and move into the complex plane well before reaching $d=3$, suggesting that their continuation to $d=3$ does not yield unitary CFTs. We then work directly in three dimensions at large but finite $N$, with $M\ll N$, initially without assuming any permutation symmetry among the $M$ scalars. By studying the RG flow, we show that these models possess a conformal manifold up to the leading nontrivial order in the $1/N$ expansion of the beta functions (which is $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$). At each point on this manifold, the scalars split into two classes that are distinguished by how they couple to the $O(N)$ vector field. We then restrict to a subspace of the conformal manifold where there is an additional symmetry under permutations of the scalars within each class. We prove that in a domain of this subspace, all $M$ scalars acquire thermal expectation values such that the symmetry under sign flips of the scalars is spontaneously broken at all nonzero temperatures. This provides a novel class of large $N$ CFTs that exhibit a rich pattern of persistent symmetry breaking at nonzero temperatures.

</details>


### [115] [Higher anomalies, compressing SPTs and cohomology operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00170)
*Shani Nadir Meynet,Elias Riedel Gårding*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了上同调运算的悬置映射，并将其应用于量子场论和格点系统中反常和广义对称性高群结构的维度约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有离散p-形式对称性的量子系统中，研究将对称性缺陷限制在时空的余维-q子空间或等效地将规范场限制在厚度趋于零的薄层中时，对称性的性质如何变化。

Method: 利用Cartan和Moore开创的Eilenberg-MacLane空间链DGA的小模型，并通过p进级数改进模p链收缩到p局部整数，结合软件包emcm自动化计算，并展示如何在此框架中计算杯积和某些cup-i积。

Result: 确定了降维后对称性的反常（即原对称性的高阶反常）以及两个形成高群结构的对称性在降维后的高群结构；证明了q次迭代的上同调悬置Ω在稳定范围内是同构，并消去混合反常。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解量子系统中对称性维度约化提供了系统性的上同调工具，并通过计算表格和软件实现推动了相关理论的实际应用。

Abstract: We explicitly compute and tabulate the suspension map for cohomology operations, applying it to the dimensional reduction of anomalies and higher group structures of generalised symmetries in quantum field theories and lattice systems.
  In a quantum system with a discrete $p$-form symmetry, we can restrict the symmetry defects to a codimension-$q$ subspace of spacetime, or equivalently confine the gauge field to a slab whose thickness approaches zero. This results in a $(p-q)$-form symmetry on the subspace. We determine the fate of two important properties of symmetries under this process. First, we compute the anomaly of the reduced symmetry, or the higher anomaly of the original symmetry, which acts as an obstruction to higher gauging and onsiteability. Second, for two symmetries forming a higher-group structure, encoded as a Postnikov class, we compute the reduced higher-group structure, which may or may not be trivial. Both cases are captured by the $q$-fold iteration of the cohomology suspension $Ω$, also known as the loop functor or transgression, in the cohomology of Eilenberg--MacLane spaces. Classical results state that $Ω$ is an isomorphism in a stable range and annihilates mixed anomalies.
  To calculate $Ω$ over a base ring which is not a field, we make use of small models for the chain DGAs of Eilenberg--MacLane spaces, pioneered by Cartan and Moore, whose results we review and extend. The critical added step is the improvement of a mod $p$ chain contraction to the $p$-local integers, obtained via a $p$-adic series. We automate the computations in a software package \texttt{emcm}, and provide extensive tables of the results. Finally, to make contact with explicit cochain formulations of cohomology operations, we demonstrate how to calculate cup products and certain cup-$i$ products in this framework.

</details>


### [116] [Anomaly Matching between Topological Superconductors and $\mathcal{N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00214)
*Christopher W. Murphy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了四维N=8超引力理论中Z4对称性的反常消除问题，发现其费米子谱（8个引力微子和56个自旋1/2粒子）能完全抵消Z16边界反常，从而保持Z4对称性无反常且可规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最小超对称标准模型中，引力微子贡献-7/16的反常，阻碍Z4对称性的规范；本文旨在寻找能规避此障碍的超引力理论。

Method: 利用Smith同态将超引力反常与拓扑超导体的Dai-Freed反常联系起来，并分析SU(8) R-对称性下的费米子表示及Z4结构的反常匹配。

Result: N=8超引力的8个引力微子和56个自旋1/2粒子精确饱和Z16边界反常，对任意允许的Z4结构均成立，且与连续SU(8)反常消失一致。

Conclusion: 在最大超引力中，Z4对称性即使存在8个引力微子仍保持无反常并可规范，为统一理论中的离散对称性提供了新可能。

Abstract: The boundary of a $3{+}1$-dimensional topological superconductor carries a $\mathbb{Z}_{16}$-valued global (Dai-Freed) anomaly, famously matched by the $16$ fermions of a Standard Model generation once a certain $\mathbb{Z}_4$ symmetry is gauged. In a supergravity completion, though, a gravitino contributes $-7/16$ rather than $\pm1/16$, obstructing this $\mathbb{Z}_4$ in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model. We observe that four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity evades this fate. Its fermions, eight gravitini in the $\mathbf 8$ and fifty-six spin-$\tfrac12$ states in the $\mathbf{56}=Λ^3\mathbf 8$ of the $SU(8)$ R-symmetry, saturate the anomaly. The cancellation holds for every admissible $\mathbb{Z}_4$ structure, is consistent with the vanishing of the continuous $SU(8)$ anomaly, and follows from a Smith homomorphism identifying the supergravity anomaly with that of the topological superconductor. In maximal supergravity this $\mathbb{Z}_4$ stays anomaly-free and gaugeable despite its eight gravitini. All numbers are reproduced by an open-source library described in the Supplemental Material.

</details>


### [117] [Topologically induced deformations of differential forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00353)
*J. M. Hoff da Silva,J. M. B. Matzenbacher,R. da Rocha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种三阶幂零的变形外微分算子，它引发局部标架的非完整重缩放，并导致上同调结构的分岔；证明了Poincaré引理在两个闭/恰当范畴中均失效；并将该框架应用于四维有效场论，通过纯几何机制（无需额外空间维度）生成电弱尺度与普朗克尺度间的等级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受自旋结构变形及其几何含义的启发，研究一种变形的外微分算子。

Method: 研究一种三阶幂零的变形外微分算子，分析其诱导的非完整标架重缩放及上同调结构分岔，并证明Poincaré引理在两个闭/恰当范畴中的失效；随后将该几何框架应用于四维有效场论。

Result: 发现该变形导数导致两种不同的闭性与恰当性概念，且Poincaré引理在两者中均不成立；在四维有效场论中，常数形变参数饱和可纯几何地生成电弱尺度与普朗克尺度间的等级结构。

Conclusion: 高阶微分结构可导致丰富的新几何与拓扑现象，并为基本物理尺度等级问题提供无需额外维数的纯几何解释路径。

Abstract: Motivated by deformations of spin structures and their geometric implications, we study a deformed exterior derivative, which is a third-order nilpotent operator. This higher-order differential structure induces an anholonomic rescaling of the local frame and leads to a bifurcation of the associated cohomological structure, with two distinct notions of closedness and exactness. We prove that the Poincaré Lemma fails in both sectors, so that closed deformed forms need not be exact even locally. We then apply the resulting framework to a four-dimensional effective field theory and show that a constant saturation of the deformation parameter can generate a hierarchy between the electroweak and fundamental Planck scales through a purely geometric mechanism, without introducing extra spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [118] [Iso-$μ/T$ holographic entropy and its attractor for a strongly coupled quantum fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00558)
*W. Barreto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过数值方法研究了Bjorken膨胀的强耦合R荷等离子体，构建了保持化学势与温度比（μ/T）恒定的演化曲线，从而揭示熵产生消失期与主能量条件违反之间的关联，并部分刻画了高温高密强耦合量子流体的等μ/T熵密度流体动力学吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强耦合等离子体中熵产生消失期与主能量条件违反之间的关联，并建立与N=4 SYM等离子体（μ/T=0）的可比基准。

Method: 采用数值方法演化Bjorken R荷等离子体，调节初始能量密度ε₀和电荷密度ρ₀以生成恒定μ/T的演化曲线，并分析其熵密度演化行为。

Result: 成功构造出保持μ/T恒定的Bjorken R荷等离子体演化路径；发现所有Bekenstein-Hawking熵密度均趋近于相同μ/T构型；为熵产生与主能量条件违反之间的相关性提供了更清晰的数值证据；部分刻画了等μ/T熵密度的流体动力学吸引子。

Conclusion: 熵产生消失期确与主能量条件违反密切相关；恒定μ/T演化框架不仅增强了与零化学势情形的可比性，还揭示了强耦合量子流体中存在一类由μ/T参数化的流体动力学吸引子结构。

Abstract: Numerical evidence has shown that a period of vanishing entropy production during far-from-equilibrium stages induces subsequent violations of the dominant energy condition in strongly coupled plasmas. This behavior also appears to hold for a Bjorken-expanding, hot and dense strongly coupled quantum fluid. In this context, it has been established that the chemical potential-to-temperature ratio ($μ/T$) in the medium increases with higher initial charge density, $ρ_0$, and/or lower initial energy density, $\varepsilon_0$. Here, we present a numerical method to evolve Bjorken R-charged plasmas by varying $(\varepsilon_0,ρ_0)$ in order to generate a curve that preserves a constant $μ/T$. This allows us to address the case in which all Bekenstein-Hawking entropy densities evolve towards configurations with the same $μ/T$. This approach enables a more direct comparison with the $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM plasma case ($μ/T=0$) and, consequently, provides clearer evidence of the correlation between entropy production and violations of the dominant energy condition. Moreover, this procedure offers a characterization (albeit numerical and partial) of the iso$-μ/T$ entropy density hydrodynamic attractor for hot and dense strongly coupled quantum fluids.

</details>


### [119] [Carroll Limit of $\mathcal{O}(F^2)$ $\text{D}_p$-branes from Kaluza--Klein-like Null reduction in the Polyakov Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00797)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Polyakov-KK方法构建了Dp-膜在O(F²)截断下的电与磁Carroll极限，揭示其作为约束系统的独特性质，并指出固定世界面帧的预冻结在Dirac程序中不合法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解Carroll对称性下Dp-膜的动力学行为，特别是其与null string及DBI理论的区别，需严格遵循Dirac约束分析程序，避免对辅助世界面帧的不当处理。

Method: 采用Polyakov-KK路线：结合固定光锥动量的单模Kaluza-Klein约化与保留辅助世界面帧的Dirac分类；系统分析电/磁两类Carroll极限下的约束结构、Weyl对称性（χ）实现障碍及自由度计数。

Result: 发现p≥2时Carroll Dp-膜不能作为固定帧约束系统处理；电家族的障碍源于U(1)物质场，磁家族则源于标量动能密度的密度权结构；χ对称性仅在标量动能密度层面实现，全作用量中被U(1)导致的Gauss等括号阻碍；电/磁家族在P₋≠0时分别有d−2和d−3自由度，P₋=0时各减1；理论既非null string也非原DBI理论。

Conclusion: Carroll Dp-膜在电/磁极限下构成新型约束系统，其正确处理必须保留世界面帧至Dirac括号消去阶段，且其对称性与自由度结构显著区别于已知张力为零或null弦模型。

Abstract: We construct the electric and magnetic Carroll limits of the $\mathcal{O}(F^2)$-truncated $\text{D}_p$-brane via the Polyakov--KK route, which combines a single-mode Kaluza--Klein-like reduction at fixed light-cone momentum with a Dirac classification that keeps the auxiliary worldvolume frame. Unlike the $U(1)$-free ILST string, for $p\geq2$ the Carrollian D$_{p}$-brane cannot be treated as a consistent fixed-frame constrained system after the Dirac classification. In the electric family the obstruction is directly produced by the worldvolume $U(1)$ matter sector, while in the magnetic family the frame retention is forced by the scalar kinetic's density-weight structure rather than by the $U(1)$ currents. The frame must therefore be retained through the classification and eliminated afterwards by Dirac brackets. Freezing it beforehand is not a legitimate step in the Dirac procedure. The embedding scalar sector inherits the Carroll--Weyl $χ$ structure at the level of the ILST kinetic density, which is $χ$-invariant, whereas the full scalar action is only $χ$-covariant. The local extension of $χ$ to the full matter sector is obstructed by the $U(1)$ sector through the Gauss, circle and $A_-$-gradient brackets. The electric family keeps the global rescaling as a weak symmetry, while the magnetic family fails even for a global parameter. For $P_{-}\neq0$ the electric and magnetic families have $d-2$ and $d-3$ degrees of freedom, and the restored local circle at $P_{-}=0$ removes one further degree of freedom in each family. These features identify the resulting theories as constrained systems distinct from both the null string and the parent $\mathcal{O}(F^2)$ DBI theory, and we also discuss their on-shell interpretation and relation to tensionless/null strings.

</details>


### [120] [Fuchsian Resonance and a Horizon-to-Boundary Dictionary for Pole-Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00942)
*Yoon-Seok Choun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞或黑膜背景下的极点跳过现象，通过分析径向方程的Frobenius解和共振条件，建立了视界局部共振与边界源-响应结构之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极点跳过现象中视界局部机制如何全局地影响边界上的格林函数行为，特别是源与响应零点的0/0结构。

Method: 利用二阶标量径向方程的Frobenius级数展开，分析共振阶下的可解性条件，研究非共振入射解在趋近共振时的奇点消除与极限行为，并连接视界光滑性与边界连接系数。

Result: 证明了共振时Frobenius递推矩阵奇异、对数项消失、存在两个独立光滑视界解三者等价；并揭示了同一可解性函数控制视界共振与边界源-响应零点结构及不同趋近方向所选共振解。

Conclusion: 建立了从视界Fuchsian共振到边界源-响应0/0结构的严格字典关系，统一了极点跳过的局部与全局描述，并指出参数变化方向决定所选共振解。

Abstract: Pole-skipping occurs at special complex frequencies and momenta where the retarded Green function of a black-hole or black-brane background is not uniquely defined. The local near-horizon mechanism is well known: at resonance, a Frobenius recurrence matrix loses rank and the space of smooth horizon solutions enlarges. We address the global question of how two boundary-normalized solutions enter this resonant horizon solution space.
  For a general second-order scalar radial equation on a nonextremal background analytic near the horizon, the Frobenius recurrence at the resonant order yields a solvability condition. We prove that its vanishing is equivalent to singularity of the horizon recurrence matrix, absence of the logarithmic Frobenius term, and existence of two independent smooth horizon solutions. Away from resonance, the source zero is equivalent to horizon smoothness of the response-normalized solution, while the response zero is equivalent to horizon smoothness of the source-normalized solution.
  We then analyze the parameter-dependent simple pole of the nonresonant ingoing solution as resonance is approached. After removing this singularity, its resonant limit is proportional to the larger-root Frobenius solution, with proportionality factor given by the same solvability function. Continuing to the boundary shows that the same condition is equivalent to simultaneous vanishing of the two regularized boundary connection coefficients. This establishes a horizon-to-boundary dictionary between local Fuchsian resonance and the boundary source-response 0/0 structure of pole-skipping.
  Finally, different directions of approach can select different resonant solutions, with the selection governed by the first parameter variation of the same solvability function.

</details>


### [121] [Logarithmic corrections to the entropy for near-extremal rotating black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01039)
*Nabamita Banerjee,Koustubh Guha,Muktajyoti Saha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了近极端旋转黑洞的量子修正熵，特别是对数面积项和对数温度修正项，并在三维、四维和五维中分析了旋转零模的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子修正对Bekenstein-Hawking熵引入对数面积项，其系数由无质量场谱决定；近极端黑洞熵还含对数温度修正，其系数由对应极端黑洞的零模决定。本文聚焦于旋转零模在该修正中的作用。

Method: 分析三维BTZ、四维及五维BMPV近极端旋转黑洞的熵修正，显式构造五维BMPV黑洞的极端零模，并结合宇宙学常数尺度ℓ作为独立参数考察其影响。

Result: 验证了三维BTZ黑洞结果与微观描述一致；发现极端情形下存在额外的log ℓ熵修正项；显式构造并利用五维BMPV黑洞所有极端零模计算了熵的对数温度修正。

Conclusion: 旋转零模在近极端黑洞熵的量子修正中起关键作用；宇宙学常数尺度ℓ可作为独立参数引入新的对数修正；该框架统一处理了不同维度下对数熵修正的起源。

Abstract: An interesting feature of quantum corrections to the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy is the appearance of logarithmic area terms, whose coefficients are governed by the spectrum of massless fields. For near-extremal black holes, the entropy also receives a logarithmic temperature corrections, whose coefficient is determined by the zero modes of the corresponding extremal black holes. In this paper, we study near-extremal rotating black holes in three, four, and five dimensions with an important focus on rotational zero modes. For three-dimensional BTZ black holes, our results are in agreement with the microscopic description. Furthermore, by treating the cosmological constant scale \(\ell\) as an independent parameter, we find an additional \(\log \ell\) correction to the entropy at extremality. For the five-dimensional BMPV black hole, we explicitly construct all the extremal zero modes and use them to evaluate the log temperature corrections to the entropy.

</details>


### [122] [Galilean Kalb-Ramond Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01097)
*Aditya Mehra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由Kalb-Ramond二形式场的伽利略极限及其对称性，采用Inönü-Wigner收缩和零约化两种方法，分别得到在D=6时具有无限维伽利略共形代数不变性的非等价电/磁极限，以及具有局域伽利略拉格朗日量但仅全局对称的构造，并计算了两种构造下的两点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自由Kalb-Ramond二形式场在非相对论极限（伽利略极限）下的行为及对称结构，以深化对非相对论共形场论的理解。

Method: 采用两种方法：1) Inönü-Wigner收缩——通过时空坐标与二形式场的标度变换，导出电、磁两类伽利略极限；2) 零约化——从D+1维理论降维至D维，获得局域伽利略拉格朗日量。

Result: Inönü-Wigner收缩下，电/磁极限方程在D=6时均具有完整无限维伽利略共形代数不变性；零约化给出仅具全局对称（不含高阶Witt模）的局域理论；并基于对称性计算了两点函数，且发现零约化结果可通过截断映射到磁极限结果。

Conclusion: 两种方法揭示了伽利略极限下不同对称性实现方式：无限维代数不变性（收缩法）与有限维全局对称（零约化），并统一于两点函数结构，为非相对论高阶张量场论提供了重要范例。

Abstract: In this paper, we build the Galilean limit of the free Kalb-Ramond two-form and also study the symmetries. Two different methods are discussed. The first is an Inönü-Wigner contraction. In this method, we take the scaling of space-time coordinates and the two-form field. The relativistic equations boil down to two inequivalent limits, electric and magnetic. In both, the equations of motion come out to be invariant under the full infinite-dimensional Galilean conformal algebra precisely in D=6. The second method is the null-reduction. In this, we start from a theory in D+1 dimensions and end up with a theory in D dimensions. This method yields a local Galilean Lagrangian. Here, the action is invariant under the global generators (boosts, rotations, translations and scale transformations) but not under higher Witt modes of the Galilean conformal algebra. We also calculate the two-point functions in both constructions from the boost, scale and rotation Ward identities, and by exhibiting the truncation that maps the null-reduction correlators onto those of the magnetic limit.

</details>


### [123] [Perturbative Double-Scalar Hair on Planar Black Holes in AdS$_{4}$ Einstein-Scalar Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01332)
*Sangheon Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个四维爱因斯坦引力耦合两个标量场的双标量‘有毛’平面黑洞解，其中势能来自ABJM理论在十一维超引力中的截断。通过微扰方法（以第二个标量振幅ε展开至二阶），得到解析解；发现第二个标量无法自发产生（必带源），其对偶无关算符（Δ=4）导致二阶响应出现对数跑动的凝聚态；温度在固定熵下有确定的二阶负向偏移（δT/T₀ ≈ −0.345 ε²）；部分输运系数保持共形不变，而声速和自由能则依赖于重整化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多标量场背景下引力/规范对偶中‘有毛’黑洞的构造及其热力学与输运性质，特别是当存在无关算符时的非平凡响应行为与重整化方案依赖性问题。

Method: 基于已知单标量有毛黑膜解，对第二个标量φ₋以小参数ε进行微扰展开，求解至ε²阶的耦合爱因斯坦-标量方程；利用超几何函数精确求得线性化φ₋分布；通过闭式解析方法获得完整度规与主标量φ₊的回响，并将扰动方程约化为两个Pöschl-Teller问题。

Result: 1) φ₋无法自发产生（horizon-regular解恒含源）；2) 二阶响应呈现对数跑动凝聚，斜率确定为√21/70 Cs²；3) 几何温度偏移δT/T₀ ≈ −0.345 ε²（固定熵下方案无关）；4) 度规与φ₊回响获闭式解；5) 三个输运系数严格共形，声速与自由能方案依赖。

Conclusion: 在含无关对偶算符的双标量引力模型中，‘有毛’结构受强约束：自发对称破缺被禁止，但出现对数跑动的非平衡响应；热力学量如温度偏移具有普适性，而动力学量如声速则暴露重整化不确定性；结果凸显了无关算符在AdS/CFT中对临界现象与有效描述的深刻影响。

Abstract: We construct a two-scalar hairy planar black hole in four-dimensional Einstein gravity coupled to two scalars whose potential descends from the ABJM consistent truncation of eleven-dimensional supergravity. Starting from the known single-scalar hairy black brane, we switch on the second scalar $\phim$ perturbatively in its amplitude $\eps$ and solve the coupled Einstein-scalar system to order $\eps^{2}$. The linearized $\phim$ profile is an exact hypergeometric function, independent of the $\pp$ hair, whose horizon-regular branch always carries a source, so there is no spontaneous $\phim$ hair. Because the dual operator $\mathcal{O}_{-}$ is irrelevant ($Δ=4$), the second-order response develops a logarithmically running condensate with a closed-form, scheme-independent slope $\tfrac{\sqrt{21}}{70}C_{s}^{2}$. The renormalized free energy is scheme dependent, but the geometric temperature shift $δT/T_{0}\approx-0.345\,\eps^{2}$ at fixed entropy is not. The full metric and $\pp$ back-reaction is obtained in closed form, diagonalizing into two Pöschl-Teller problems with $δ\pp=\tfrac{6}{\sqrt7}\,δH$. Three transport coefficients of the dual fluid remain exactly conformal to this order, while the sound speed, like the free energy, is scheme dependent.

</details>


### [124] [Lagrangian varieties from $q$-matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01454)
*Victor Mishnyakov,Maxim Zabzine*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种q-形变矩阵模型（Chern-Simons、q-Laguerre和q-Gaussian），利用超可积性显式求解，并将逆特征多项式的单点与两点函数解释为复环面中拉格朗日子簇的量子化，提出了新的几何框架用于分析大N展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立矩阵模型相关量的几何诠释，特别是将逆特征多项式关联函数与复环面上的拉格朗日子簇联系起来，以深化对大N极限和拓扑弦理论的理解。

Method: 利用矩阵模型的超可积性求解Chern-Simons、q-Laguerre和q-Gaussian模型；引入反辛双有理对合等几何工具，将关联函数解释为(ℂ*)²和(ℂ*)⁴中拉格朗日子簇的量子化。

Result: 首次为q-Laguerre和q-Gaussian模型建立了逆特征多项式关联函数的几何诠释，揭示了反辛双有理对合的关键作用，并构建了适用于 semiclassical 大-N 展开的几何框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为q-形变矩阵模型提供了统一的几何视角，暗示其与开弦拓扑理论存在潜在联系，但二者确切关系仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We consider three specific $q$-deformed matrix models (the Chern-Simons, $q$-Laguerre, and $q$-Gaussian matrix models) which can be solved explicitly using the property of superintegrability. We show that one- and two-point functions of inverse characteristic polynomials can be interpreted as quantizations of Lagrangian subvarieties in $(\mathbb{C}^*)^2$ and $(\mathbb{C}^*)^4$, respectively. While such a geometric picture is expected for the Chern-Simons matrix model, our results for the $q$-Laguerre and $q$-Gaussian models are new and exhibit additional features. In particular, specific anti-symplectic birational involutions play an essential role in the construction. This reformulation provides a geometric framework for analyzing the semiclassical, large-$N$ expansion of matrix-model correlators. This picture is suggestive of the geometry underlying open topological strings, although the two constructions are not identical and the precise relation between them remains to be understood.

</details>


### [125] [Non-symmetric triads and Baker-Akhiezer functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01517)
*A. Mironov,A. Morozov,A. Popolitov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称Macdonald多项式在特殊参数值下的分解性质，揭示了其与Baker-Akhiezer函数及Cherednik哈密顿量本征态解之间的深刻联系，并构建了非对称三元组结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对称与非对称Macdonald多项式在特殊参数t=q^{-m}下的结构分解，及其与可积系统中Baker-Akhiezer函数和Cherednik哈密顿量本征解的关系。

Method: 通过分析非对称Macdonald多项式的代数结构，研究其在t=q^{-m}时的（拟）多项式BA函数分解；构造通用本征解；推广至任意t值并关联Noumi-Shiraishi级数；系统分析N=2情形。

Result: 发现非对称Macdonald多项式仍可分解为N!个不同BA函数，其和构成Cherednik哈密顿量的普适本征解；建立含N!个分支、每支含N!个三元组的‘非对称三元组’结构，其中N!(N-1)!个独立；详述N=2情形。

Conclusion: 非对称Macdonald多项式具有丰富的分层结构，其与BA函数和Noumi-Shiraishi级数共同构成统一的非对称可积结构框架，拓展了经典对称理论。

Abstract: The symmetric Macdonald polynomial at peculiar values of parameter $t=q^{-m}$, $m\in\mathbb{Z}_{\ge 0}$ is naturally split into non-symmetric parts, which are the (quasi)polynomial Baker-Akhiezer (BA) functions. One may think this is due to symmetricity, and one just picks up this way non-symmetric parts already containing all the information. However, we demonstrate that, in the case of {\bf non-symmetric} Macdonald polynomials, it still works, though each single BA function splits into $N!$ distinct (quasi)polynomial BA functions. The sum of these functions gives rise to the universal solution of the eigenstate problem for the Cherednik Hamiltonians. Extending to arbitrary values of $t$ is also immediate giving rise to counterparts of the Noumi-Shiraishi power series. Altogether, this power series and its reductions to non-symmetric Macdonald polynomials and to BA functions form a non-symmetric triad. There are $N!$ different branches of the non-symmetric triad, each branch being split into $N!$ distinct triads, and of these $(N!)^2$ triads $N!(N-1)!$ are independent. We describe in detail the simplest $N=2$ case.

</details>

### [126] [Long-distance hybrid spin-dependent and hybrid-quarkonium mixing potentials from lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00266)
*Vilija de Jonge,Paul Pütz,Antonio Vairo,Marc Wagner*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模、高统计量的格点模拟，首次将SU(3)格点规范理论中O(1/m_Q)混合自旋依赖势与混合夸克偶素混合势的计算范围从约0.5 fm扩展至约1.25 fm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有格点计算仅限于夸克-反夸克间距小于约0.5 fm的区域，缺乏更大距离下的数据，限制了对相关物理现象的理解。

Method: 采用大体积、高统计量的SU(3)格点规范理论模拟，结合多种格距和梯度流时间，以提高精度并拓展可及距离范围。

Result: 成功获得距离达约1.25 fm的混合自旋依赖势与混合夸克偶素混合势的格点结果，填补了此前未被探索的距离区间。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解重夸克系统中的自旋相关相互作用和混合效应提供了关键的非微扰格点依据，推动了强子谱学与QCD有效理论的发展。

Abstract: We present $SU(3)$ lattice gauge theory results for the $\mathcal{O}(1/m_Q)$ hybrid spin-dependent and hybrid-quarkonium mixing potentials. The only existing lattice computation of these potentials is limited to quark-antiquark separations smaller than approximately $0.5\,\text{fm}$. In this work, we extend the lattice results up to around $1.25 \, \text{fm}$ using large-volume, high-statistics simulations at several lattice spacings and gradient flow times, providing the first lattice data in this previously unexplored regime.

</details>

### [127] [Efficient searches of small signals across two-channel noisy data: a challenge in Big Data observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00013)
*Maryam Aghaei Abchouyeh,Maurice H. P. M. van Putten*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于尾部数据的高效相关性检测方法——过量概率比（EPR），通过仅对超过κσ阈值的双通道数据进行布尔化处理并相关，显著减少数据移动开销，同时保持与皮尔逊相关系数（PC）相当的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大数据分析中，移动数据是主要开销；为提升效率，需在保证信号检测灵敏度的前提下大幅减少参与相关计算的数据量。

Method: 提出Excess Probability Ratio（EPR）方法：对双通道数据分别进行κσ阈值截断得到布尔序列，再计算其联合出现概率比；并与直接互相关（DCC）和皮尔逊相关系数（PC）在多种噪声（高斯、泊松、均匀）和信号（高斯、泊松、均匀、啁啾、正弦）组合下对比评估。

Result: EPR在各类噪声-信号组合下的检测性能与PC相当，显著优于DCC；当κ≳2时，数据量减少一个数量级以上，计算效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: EPR是一种兼顾高效率与高灵敏度的相关分析新范式，特别适用于大数据观测场景（如天文、粒子物理等），可在几乎不损失探测能力的前提下极大降低计算与通信开销。

Abstract: Searching for novel small signals in noisy data is preferably pursued by correlation in two or more independently operating channels. Signals of potential interest exist in tails beyond $κσ$, where $κ$ denotes a multiple of the standard deviation $σ$ of the data. Since moving data is a major cost factor in Big Data analysis and heterogeneous computing more generally, efficiency may be optimized by restricting the correlations computation to the tails of two-channel data exceeding $κσ$. Already, a moderate value $κ\gtrsim2$ realizes a data-reduction by at least an order of magnitude. Here, we study this approach using a novel {\it Excess Probability Ratio} (EPR), correlating Boolean data resulting from tails beyond a cut-off $κσ$. We compare and rank EPR performance against conventional direct cross-correlation (DCC) and Pearson coefficient (PC), applicable to the original data with no cut-off. This benchmark is performed over different combinations of background noise Gaussian, Poisson and Uniform and signals Gaussian, Poisson, Uniform, Chirps and Sine waves. Results show performance of EPR to be comparable to that of PC, providing a new approach for significant improvements in efficiency with essentially no loss of sensitivity, relevant to the present era of Big Data observatories.

</details>

Tremblay,John ZuHone,Steven W. Allen,Antara R. Basu-Zych,Akos Bogdan,Joel N. Bregman,Tamta Burduli,Thomas Connor,Sanskriti Das,Casey DeRoo,Stephen DiKerby,Paul A. Draghis,Martin Elvis,Giuseppina Fabbiano,Ralf K. Heilmann,Jimmy A. Irwin,Amruta Jaodand,Margarita Karovska,Vinay L. Kashyap,Anthony A. Kerr,Caroline Kilbourne,Ralph Kraft,Jiangtao Li,Labani Mallick,Herman L. Marshall,Michael L. McCollough,Anna Ogorzałek,Frederik Paerels,Daniel Patnaude,Paul Plucinsky,David Pooley,Frederick S. Porter,Daniele Rogantini,Roger Romani,Helen R. Russell,Kazuhiro Sakai,Mark Schattenburg,Dan A. Schwartz,Malgorzata Sobolewska,Paolo Soffitta,Alexey Vikhlinin,Daniel R. Wilkins,Scott Wolk,Ka-Wah Wong,Irina Zhuravleva*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Lynx2030 SAG重新评估了Lynx X射线天文台的科学目标与技术需求，确认其在多波段与多信使时代仍具强大且增强的科学价值，尤其在探测早期黑洞、星系演化等领域；报告指出小幅升级原设计方案即可实现突破性科学成果。


<details>
