Motivation: 在天体物理学快速发展的背景下，重新评估下一代旗舰X射线望远镜Lynx的科学目标与技术驱动因素，特别是应对JWST等新设施带来的新发现（如'小红点'——婴儿星系中可能的高红移吸积黑洞）所提出的X射线观测需求。

Method: 通过多个专题工作组，系统分析提升角分辨率、拓展能段覆盖、增强微量热计性能、新增观测模式及扩大视场等能力对关键科学课题的影响，并结合现有与未来设施（如JWST、Roman、SKA、LISA等）评估Lynx的独特定位。

Result: 确认Lynx类望远镜的科学必要性不仅持续存在，而且在过去十年显著增强；小幅超越原参考设计即可解锁变革性科学能力；Lynx将在2030年代多波段、多信使天文中发挥不可替代的核心作用。

Conclusion: Lynx任务愿景依然坚实——提供前所未有的高温高能宇宙视图，引领未来数十年高能天体物理的发展；其核心架构稳健，升级路径明确可行。

Abstract: The Lynx2030 Science Analysis Group (SAG) was convened to reassess the scientific goals and technical drivers of the Lynx mission concept amid a rapidly evolving astrophysics landscape. Building on the original Lynx Concept Study, the SAG examined how recent discoveries, emerging facilities, and advances in instrumentation influence the scientific opportunities for a next-generation flagship X-ray observatory. Through focused working groups, the SAG investigated the scientific impact of enhanced capabilities: (i) improved angular resolution, (ii) broader bandpass coverage, (iii) an enhanced microcalorimeter, (iv) new capabilities and observing modes, and (v) larger fields of view. Across a broad range of topics, from the formation of the first black holes and the evolution of galaxies to the baryon cycle, compact objects, stellar explosions, multi-messenger astrophysics, and the dynamic high-energy Universe, the SAG finds that the scientific motivation for a Lynx-class observatory remains compelling and, in many areas, has significantly strengthened over the past decade, prominently through JWST's discovery of the "Little Red Dots", likely massive accreting black holes in infant galaxies whose nature is fundamentally an X-ray question. This report shows that modest extensions beyond the original Lynx design reference mission can unlock transformative science while preserving the observatory's core architecture. Powerful current and future facilities such as Roman, Rubin, JWST, SKA, ngVLA, LISA, and NewAthena highlight the unique role a high-angular-resolution, high-throughput X-ray observatory would play in the multi-wavelength and multi-messenger ecosystem of the 2030s and beyond. The findings of the Lynx2030 SAG confirm Lynx's central vision: an unprecedented view of the hot and energetic Universe, enabling discoveries that will define high-energy astrophysics in the coming decades.

</details>


### [129] [Detectability-Aware Screening of Post-Merger Gravitational-Wave Damping Anomalies in Real Detector Noise: A Graph-Spectral Approach with Empirical Null Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00095)
*Ruslan Alaskarov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法，用于在真实LIGO Livingston噪声中筛查简化的并合后引力波阻尼异常；第一阶段利用常Q变换构建时频图并以主特征值作为可检测性统计量，第二阶段对通过第一阶段的候选信号用非线性最小二乘法估计有效阻尼时间，并在真实噪声测试中取得优异性能（AUC=0.943，TPR=0.879@FPR=0.012）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升在真实探测器噪声背景下对二元中子星并合后引力波信号中阻尼特征的统计可检测性与量化评估能力，弥补完整物理波形建模困难与噪声干扰带来的挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：Stage 1基于白化候选窗的Constant-Q时频表示构建局部时频图，用主特征值作为检测统计量，并在实测噪声上经验校准其零分布；Stage 2对Stage-1通过的候选者，使用非线性最小二乘拟合和可解释的岭/能量衰减估计器估算有效阻尼时间，阈值预先固定；辅以功率比较与tile-shuffling检验评估图统计量性能。

Result: 在独立保留的真实噪声测试块上，Stage-2非线性最小二乘阻尼时间得分达到AUC = 0.943，TPR = 0.879（FPR = 0.012），显著优于仅依赖功率的校准基线。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架为在高噪声真实数据中稳健识别并量化简化后并合阻尼信号提供了可行且高性能的证明概念，支持后续向更复杂物理波形扩展。

Abstract: A two-stage methodological proof of concept for screening simplified post-merger gravitational-wave damping anomalies in real LIGO Livingston noise is presented. The injected signal is a controlled damped-sinusoid proxy rather than a physically complete binary-neutron-star post-merger waveform; it is used to test statistical detectability and damping-time scoring under real-noise calibration. Stage 1 constructs a local time-frequency graph from Constant-Q representations of whitened candidate windows and uses the leading eigenvalue as a detectability statistic, with its null distribution calibrated empirically on real detector noise. Stage 2 estimates an effective damping-time score for Stage-1-passing candidates using nonlinear least squares and an interpretable ridge/energy-decay estimator, with thresholds fixed before final held-out evaluation. The matched-filter SNR convention is checked with synthetic Gaussian noise, while the graph statistic is assessed through power-comparison and tile-shuffling tests. On a held-out real-noise test block, the nonlinear least-squares Stage-2 score gives AUC = 0.943 and TPR = 0.879 at FPR = 0.012, outperforming a calibrated power-only baseline.

</details>


### [130] [The promise of self-supervised and active learning for Strong Lens discovery: Astronomaly applied to KiDS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00154)
*Margherita Grespan,Aprajita Verma,Michelle Lochner,Koketso Mohale,Verlon Etsebeth,Duncan Bowden*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需模拟训练数据的人机协同主动学习框架Astronomaly:PROTEGE，用于在大型巡天数据中高效发现强引力透镜（SGLs），在KiDS数据中发现了140个高置信度候选体，其中60%为新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGL发现主要依赖基于大量模拟数据训练的监督机器学习方法，存在对模拟数据依赖性强、难以覆盖真实多样性等问题；亟需一种不依赖模拟、能适应真实数据分布的新方法。

Method: 采用Astronomaly:PROTEGE人机协同主动学习框架：先用ImageNet预训练CNN提取特征，再用BYOL在KiDS数据上自监督微调；在特征嵌入空间中，通过主动学习循环选择最具信息量样本供专家标注；最后基于最近邻相似性扩展候选体。

Result: 在370万KiDS星系中，经3000次专家检验，发现34个高置信度（A/B级）SGL候选体；扩展后共得140个A/B级候选体（81个为新发现），以及超1000个C级低置信度系统；成功复现约22%已知KiDS A/B级透镜。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低对模拟数据的依赖，提升SGL发现的多样性与效率，具备在Euclid、Roman、Rubin LSST等下一代巡天中大规模应用的潜力，可有效补充现有监督方法。

Abstract: Strong gravitational lenses (SGLs) are rare systems whose discovery currently relies primarily on supervised machine learning methods trained on large simulated datasets. We present the first application of Astronomaly:PROTEGE to SGL discovery, demonstrating that a human-in-the-loop active learning framework can efficiently identify lenses in large imaging surveys without the need for simulated training data. We consider a sample of 3.7 million bright galaxies from the Kilo-Degree Survey (KiDS) DR4. Feature representations are extracted using a convolutional neural network pre-trained on the ImageNet dataset and subsequently fine-tuned on KiDS data using the self-supervised Bootstrap Your Own Latent (BYOL) framework. Within the embedding of these representations, the active learning loop of Astronomaly iteratively selects the most informative systems for expert inspection. A total of 3,000 objects are inspected across multiple rounds, yielding 34 high-quality (grade A/B) SGL candidates. On the basis that these systems occupy similar regions in the learned feature space, we expand this sample through nearest-neighbour similarity analysis. Including the active learning discoveries, we identify a total of 140 grade A/B candidates and more than 1,000 additional lower-confidence systems (grade C). Among the A/B candidates, 81 are newly identified, while approximately 22% of previously known grade A/B KiDS lenses are recovered. These results demonstrate strong potential for next-generation surveys such as Euclid, Roman, and Rubin's Legacy Survey of Space and Time. With approximately 60% of the high-quality candidates newly reported, this approach complements supervised methods by reducing reliance on simulations and enabling the discovery of a diverse population of SGLs.

</details>


### [131] [Flux jump correction in the QUBIC TES data pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00249)
*Belen Costanza,Lucas Merlo Azzolina,Claudia G. Scoccola,Nahuel Miron Granese,Elia Battistelli,Jean-Christophe Hamilton,Michel Piat,Damien Prele,Steve Torchinsky,Ismail Bekkaoui,Alessandro Coppolecchia,Paolo De Bernardis,Sofia Ferazzoli,Massimo Gervasi,Luca Gomez,Sabrina Marwede,Silvia Masi,Youssef Moulane,Alessandro Paiella,Mathias Regnier,Gustavo Romero,Federico Sanchez,Ivan Queirolo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动识别和校正QUBIC仪器校准数据中通量跳变的流程，有效恢复受影响数据，提升CMB B模极化测量质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通量跳变会导致TES-SQUID读出电子学中时序数据出现不连续，严重降低信号质量，需有效识别与校正以保障宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）精确测量。

Method: 基于QUBIC仪器多轮观测数据，构建自动化流程，用于识别和校正通量跳变，并对其统计特性进行表征，同时与仪器电子配置预期进行比对。

Result: 该流程成功校正通量跳变事件，恢复了被影响的数据，使其可用于后续CMB分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化通量跳变处理流程显著提升了QUBIC校准数据质量，为高精度CMB B模极化测量提供了可靠支持。

Abstract: Flux jumps occur in the readout electronics of Transition Edge Sensor (TES) detectors coupled to Superconducting Quantum Interference Devices (SQUIDs), as a consequence of the periodic transfer function of SQUIDs in the superconducting regime. These events manifest as discontinuities in the Time Ordered Data (TOD) and can significantly degrade the quality of the measured signals. In this work, we present an automated pipeline to identify and correct flux jumps in the calibration data of the QUBIC (Q \& U Bolometric Interferometer for Cosmology) instrument, designed to measure the B-mode polarization of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB). Using data from multiple observation campaigns, we characterize the statistical properties of flux jumps and compare them with expectations based on the electronic configuration of the instrument. The proposed pipeline successfully corrects these events, allowing the recovery of affected data and enabling their use in subsequent CMB analysis.

</details>


### [132] [Simulation and development of astrophotonic H-band nulling beam combiner for the CHARA Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00260)
*Samantha G. Morrison,Pradip Gatkine,Michael P. Fitzgerald*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为CHARA阵列开发的光子学消零光束组合器及其专用模拟器CHARM，展示了其在系外行星探测与表征方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升系外行星及暗弱伴星的直接探测能力，需克服振动、热膨胀等系统不稳定性噪声；利用光子芯片的小型化与主动相位控制优势，结合CHARA阵列高角分辨率特性，构建高性能消零干涉仪。

Method: 开发了面向CHARA阵列的H波段自校准光子学消零光束组合器，并构建首个专用模拟器CHARM，集成主动相位控制（基于MZI）与系统级性能仿真。

Result: 通过CHARM初步仿真验证，该光子消零器具备探测和表征多种明亮恒星伴星系统的能力，为后续科学目标与技术指标设定提供了依据。

Conclusion: 光子学消零干涉仪与CHARM模拟器的成功开发，标志着CHARA阵列迈向系外行星直接成像与表征的关键一步，也为未来空间任务（如Habitable Worlds Observatory）提供技术支撑。

Abstract: Nulling interferometry is a promising method for direct detection and characterization of faint stellar companions such as exoplanets and faint binary sources. The dominant constraint on instrument performance is instability noise from vibrations, thermal expansions, and other systematic sources. Photonic instruments have footprints of a few centimeters and enable advanced light control architectures. Astrophotonic implementations can include active phase control via Mach-Zehnder interferometers (MZIs) to actively minimize residual optical path errors at the location of beam combination. The compact size of photonic chips also makes them a promising solution for space-based applications such as the upcoming Habitable Worlds Observatory. The Center for High Angular Resolution Astronomy (CHARA) Array is equipped with the world's longest baseline in the near-infrared at 330 meters long. CHARA therefore has the most sensitive angular resolution in the near-infrared. A nuller at CHARA would have access to a novel parameter space essential for exoplanet discovery and characterization. The first step in preparing a nulling beam combiner for CHARA is to develop compelling, realistic, and well-defined science cases. Simulation suites quantify the technical requirements to reach such science goals in their respective complex systems. The CHARA Array Response Model (CHARM) is the first-ever simulator built specifically for CHARA, opening up new pathways for instrument simulation. CHARM is a flexible simulation tool of an H-band, self-calibrated astrophotonic nulling beam combiner with active phase control for the CHARA Array. In this paper, we present an update on the development of our photonic nulling beam combiner for CHARA. We include early results from CHARM, demonstrating that the photonic nuller has the potential to detect and characterize a wide range of bright stellar companion systems.

</details>


### [133] [The Hopkins Ultraviolet Telescope: A Historical and Personal Retrospective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00265)
*William P. Blair*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文回顾了霍普金斯紫外望远镜（HUT）项目的起源与发展，强调其1980年获NASA批准对约翰霍普金斯大学天体物理学发展的关键意义，并辅以作者亲身经历的飞行操作细节及历史资料存档链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录HUT项目的历史背景及其对约翰霍普金斯大学天体物理学发展的推动作用，并保存第一手飞行操作经验与历史资料。

Method: 采用历史文献回顾与作者个人回忆相结合的方式，辅以在线历史档案支持。

Result: 系统梳理了HUT项目从立项到两次航天飞机任务执行的全过程，突出了其在学科建设与NASA合作中的里程碑意义。

Conclusion: HUT项目不仅是重要的空间天文观测工具，更是推动约翰霍普金斯大学天体物理学崛起的关键契机，其历史经验具有学术与机构发展双重价值。

Abstract: This manuscript documents the inception and development of the Hopkins Ultraviolet Telescope (HUT) project, which was a component of the Astro Observatory payload that flew on two Spacelab shuttle missions in the 1990s. The discussion places the HUT project in the context of the times and highlights the importance of its acceptance by NASA in 1980 to the growth and development of astrophysics at the Johns Hopkins University. Personal recollections from the author, including a detailed description of the real time flight operations during each mission, provides an insiders view. The document includes a link to an online historical archive of supporting materials compiled in support of this effort.

</details>


### [134] [The Canadian Galactic Emission Mapper (CGEM): A Cosmic Microwave Foreground Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00357)
*Parham Zarei,Pedro Villalba-González,Mandana Amiri,Charles L. Bennett,Guinevere Berg,Mark Halpern,Gary Hinshaw,Gordon Lacy,Joshua MacEachern,Thomas J. Rennie,Shuyu van Kerkwijk,Bruce Veidt,Janet Weiland,Don Wiebe,Edward J. Wollack*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: CGEM 是一个位于加拿大彭蒂克顿的 4 米单盘射电望远镜，旨在以高保真度、低噪声绘制北天极化银河同步辐射图，为 CMB B 模前景清理提供可靠模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为CMB B-mode前景清理提供可靠的银河同步辐射模板，因在8-10 GHz频段同步辐射占主导地位。

Method: 使用4米单盘、方位对称望远镜与相干双极化辐射计，在8-10 GHz频段、1 MHz频率分辨率和0.5度角分辨率下进行全北天极化观测，并基于初期调试数据生成地图。

Result: 展示了CGEM望远镜及其辐射计的实测性能，并发布了初期调试数据生成的极化同步辐射图。

Conclusion: CGEM已初步验证其观测能力，有望为CMB研究提供高质量银河同步辐射前景模板。

Abstract: The Canadian Galactic Emission Mapper (CGEM) is a 4-meter single-dish radio telescope located at the Dominion Radio Astrophysical Observatory (DRAO) in Penticton, Canada. CGEM is designed to map polarized Galactic synchrotron emission across the entire northern sky at 8-10 GHz with 1 MHz frequency resolution and 0.5 degree angular resolution. Its goal is to obtain high-fidelity, low-noise maps of polarized Galactic synchrotron radiation, at frequencies where synchrotron dominates, in order to provide a reliable template for CMB B-mode foreground cleaning. We will show on sky performance of CGEM's single-pixel azimuthally-symmetric telescope, its coherent, dual-polarized radiometer, and show maps made from commissioning data from the first few months of operation.

</details>


### [135] [Addressing the low-wind effect with the Lyot-based low-order wavefront sensor on SCExAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00737)
*Garima Singh,Sebastien Vievard,Julien Lozi,Vincent Deo,Andre Fogal,Kyohoon Anh,Olivier Guyon,Sandrine Juillard*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的Lyot型低阶波前传感器（LLOWFS）方法，用于在SCExAO红外臂的冠状成像模式下实时测量并校正低风效应（LWE）引起的差分活塞像差，从而提升系外行星直接成像的对比度和探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低风效应（LWE）会显著降低直接成像仪器（如SCExAO）在冠状模式下的系外行星探测灵敏度，而现有波前传感技术大多无法兼容冠状成像模式，亟需一种能在该模式下有效工作的LWE校正方案。

Method: 改进并应用机器学习增强的Lyot型低阶波前传感器（LLOWFS），利用Lyot光阑反射的未用星光，在冠状成像光路下游（焦面掩模后）的重成像焦面或瞳面中感知并校正LWE引起的低阶像差（特别是差分活塞）。

Result: 首次在SCExAO红外臂冠状模式下实现了LLOWFS对LWE诱导差分活塞像差的实测与闭环控制，初步校正效果积极，验证了其在真实观测条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 机器学习增强的LLOWFS是一种兼容冠状成像模式的有效LWE校正手段，有望显著提升SCExAO等高对比度成像系统的性能，助力小角距成熟系外行星的探测。

Abstract: The Low-Wind Effect (LWE) is a well-known issue that affects the coronagraphic capabilities of instruments optimized to take direct images of exoplanets. This effect is prominent in segmented and obstructed pupils with spider arms, causing differential pistons due to phase discontinuities and, in some cases, tip-tilt errors under low-wind conditions. LWE-induced low-order errors contribute to coronagraphic leakage and reduce the exoplanet detection sensitivity of direct imaging instruments.
  Solutions to mitigate the impact of LWE have been developed in both passive mode, such as coating the spider arms with low-emissivity material, and active mode using wavefront sensors (WFS). The Subaru Coronagraphic Extreme Adaptive Optics (SCExAO) instrument at the Subaru Telescope also attempted to mitigate LWE by testing several dedicated WFS. Such techniques, when tested on SCExAO, have proven their ability to measure and correct LWE; however, most remain incompatible with SCExAO's coronagraphic imaging modes.
  Addressing the need for an efficient solution that can correct LWE in coronagraphic observing modes, we revisited the coronagraphic WFS on SCExAO, known as the Lyot-based Low-order Wavefront Sensor (LLOWFS). LLOWFS utilizes the unused starlight reflected off the Lyot stop and is a well-proven technique that can prevent coronagraphic leaks by sensing low-order errors in a re-imaged focal plane or pupil plane downstream of the focal plane mask.
  We present the machine-learned LLOWFS's initial on-sky measurement and control of the differential piston aberrations induced by the LWE downstream of a Lyot coronagraph in SCExAO's infrared arm. Preliminary LLOWFS corrections of LWE in the coronagraphic mode are encouraging and would leverage future high-contrast performance of SCExAO, enabling the detection of mature exoplanets at small angular separations.

</details>


### [136] [$\texttt{BilbyFlow}$: user-friendly neural posterior estimation for gravitational-wave astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00766)
*Liam Pinchbeck,Eric Thrane,Csaba Balazs,Paul Lasky*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BilbyFlow，一个基于神经后验估计（NPE）的快速贝叶斯推断工具，用于引力波天文学中的参数估计，显著缩短了后验采样时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯推断方法计算成本高，难以满足下一代引力波探测器高事件率和高信噪比的需求，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出BilbyFlow，将神经后验估计（NPE）集成到主流引力波分析软件Bilby中，并用其分析GWTC-3中38个高质事件，采用重要性采样评估性能。

Result: 对76%的事件实现了>1%的重要性采样效率，后验生成耗时仅3分钟至1.5小时；中位效率达7%，与DINGO相当；部分事件效率极低，耗时长达35小时。

Conclusion: BilbyFlow是加速引力波贝叶斯推断的有力工具，开源且易安装，后续将重点提升重要性采样效率以实现分钟级稳定运行。

Abstract: Bayesian inference plays a central role in the new field of gravitational-wave astronomy. However, traditional Bayesian inference with stochastic samplers is computationally expensive, taking hours to days per event. Transformative changes are therefore required to enable the science of next-generation observatories whose event rates and signal-to-noise ratios will increase significantly over the current generation. Recent work has shown that neural posterior estimation (NPE) is a promising path forward. A neural net is trained to approximate the posterior distribution of gravitational-wave parameters, allowing generation of posterior samples in a fraction of the time required by stochastic samplers. In this work, we introduce $\texttt{BilbyFlow}$, which harnesses the power of NPE in the popular $\texttt{Bilby}$ code suite. We use $\texttt{BilbyFlow}$ to analyze a subset of 38 high-mass events from the third LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-3). For 29 events (76\%), we obtained an importance-sampling efficiency $>$1%, allowing us to produce reliable posterior distributions within 3 min - 1.5 hours. For the other events, with importance-sampling efficiency $\ll$1%, the run time can be as long as 35 hours. We achieve a median importance-sampling efficiency of 7%, which is roughly comparable to the $\texttt{DINGO}$ package. We aim to significantly improve this efficiency with further development to make the runtime more reliably $O(\text{min})$. $\texttt{BilbyFlow}$ is open source and $\texttt{pip}$-installable.

</details>


### [137] [Exploring reinforcement learning to enhance focal-plane wavefront control for vortex coronagraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01199)
*Iremsu Taskin,Jalo Nousiainen,Gilles Orban de Xivry,Olivier Absil,Markus Kasper*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合焦点平面波前传感与强化学习（RL）的新算法PO4NCPA，用于校正地面望远镜高对比度成像中由非共光路像差（NCPAs）引起的波前畸变，已在METIS仪器仿真中验证其对标量和矢量涡旋日冕仪的有效性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应光学（AO）系统无法校正非共光路像差（NCPAs），而NCPAs严重限制高对比度成像性能，亟需新方法在焦面直接感知并校正此类像差。

Method: 提出PO4NCPA算法，融合焦点平面波前传感、序列相位分集（解决相位模糊）与强化学习策略，在仿真环境中针对METIS仪器进行测试。

Result: PO4NCPA在METIS仿真中成功校正NCPAs，且兼容标量与矢量涡旋日冕仪，展现出良好鲁棒性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 该方法为地面HCI系统中NCPA校正提供了一种无需额外硬件、基于焦面反馈的端到端智能解决方案，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: High Contrast Imaging (HCI) on ground-based telescopes suffers from phase aberrations on the observed wavefront caused by atmospheric turbulence. Adaptive Optics (AO) systems are adept at correcting these aberrations, but fall short in the correction of non-common path aberrations (NCPAs). NCPAs arise because the wavefront sensor (WFS) measures and corrects a wavefront that is different from that affecting the science images, thus requiring additional intervention. This work makes use of focal-plane wavefront sensing and reinforcement learning (RL) to address the wavefront aberrations caused by NCPAs. The PO4NCPA algorithm utilizes sequential phase diversity to address phase ambiguities and is tested on a simulation designed for the Mid-infrared ELT Imager and Spectrograph (METIS) instrument. In this paper, we present the performance of PO4NCPA with scalar and vector vortex coronagraphs to demonstrate its flexibility.

</details>


### [138] [4MOST Low Resolution Spectrographs Characterization in Chile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01219)
*Florence Laurent,Didier Boudon,Eric Daguise,Remi Giroud,Aurelien Jarno,Alexandre Jeanneau,Andreas Kelz,Jens-Kristian Krogager,Victor Mauger-Vauglin,Jean-Emmanuel Migniau,Matthew Lehnert,Flora Paganelli,Arlette Pecontal,Emmanuel Pecontal,Alban Remillieux,Johan Richard,Allar Saviauk*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了4MOST低分辨率光谱仪（LRS-A和LRS-B）在智利ESO帕拉纳尔天文台的成功测试、安装与技术调试，涵盖其集成过程、性能表现及流程优化，以满足顶层技术指标要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持4MOST仪器在银河系考古学、高能物理、星系演化和宇宙学等核心科学目标的实现，需确保其低分辨率光谱仪具备高可靠性与高性能。

Method: 开展LRS-A和LRS-B从抵达集成大厅到望远镜安装全过程的组装、集成与技术调试；采用离轴施密特准直器、分色镜分光、F/1.73相机与6k×6k CCD探测器等光学设计，并优化操作流程以提升性能。

Result: 成功完成两台低分辨率光谱仪的现场安装与技术调试（截至2025年10月18日），验证了其2436根光纤（含三通道、370–950 nm、R > 4000）、大视场（~4.1 deg²）及高光学效率等关键性能。

Conclusion: CRAL主导研制的4MOST低分辨率光谱仪已通过全面技术验证，具备开展大规模巡天观测的工程可行性，为后续科学运行奠定坚实基础。

Abstract: 4MOST, the 4m Multi Object Spectroscopic Telescope, is the optical, fibre-fed, MOS facility for the VISTA telescope at ESO's Paranal Observatory in Chile. Its main science drivers are in the fields of galactic archeology, high-energy physics, galaxy evolution and cosmology. The 4MOST consortium consists of several institutes in Europe and Australia under leadership of the Leibniz-Institut fur Astrophysik Potsdam (AIP). This paper focuses on the successful testing, installation and technical commissioning of the Low Resolution Spectrographs (LRS-A and B) for the 4MOST instrument at ESO's Paranal Observatory, Chile. This work was completed on October 18. 2025. Details on the assembly, integration, and performance of both 4MOST spectrographs from their arrival in the integration hall through to the telescope installation are provided. Attention is given to the optimization of procedures implemented to enhance performance and meet the expected top-level requirements. The 4MOST LRS features 2436 fibres split into two low-resolution spectrographs LRS-A and LRS-B (1624 fibres, three arms, 370-950 nm, R > 4000) and one high-resolution spectrograph (812 fibres, three arms, ~44-69 nm coverage each, R > 18000). The fibre positioner covers a hexagonal field of view of ~4.1 deg2. The fibers are 85 μm core with an output beam at f/3. The Centre de Recherche Astrophysique de Lyon (CRAL) had the full responsibility for the two low-resolution spectrographs. Each of them is composed of an off-axis Schmidt collimator that produces a 200 mm beam, which is split into three spectral arms by dichroics and directed to F/1.73 cameras with standard 6k x 6k 15 microns pixel CCD detectors.

</details>


### [139] [HEL1OS on Aditya-L1 Mission: Operations, Data Processing and Monitoring of Sun in Hard X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01307)
*Ravishankar B. T.,Manju Sudhakar,Srikar Paavan Tadepalli,Kiran Kumar Kalisetti,Madhavan R. Menon,Anand Jain,Reenu Palawat,Brajpal Singh,Rethika T.,Priyanka Upadhyay,Prapti Mittal,Amit Purohit,Srikanth M.,Anuj Nandi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了印度Aditya-L1太阳探测任务上HEL1OS硬X射线谱仪的在轨运行、标定验证、数据处理流程及首批科学成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现对太阳硬X射线辐射（8–150 keV）的持续、高灵敏度监测，利用日地L1点独特观测位置优势，需全面验证HEL1OS仪器性能并建立可靠的数据处理与发布体系。

Method: 开展2023年10月底启动的在轨调试（commissioning）与至2024年6月底完成的性能验证（PV），全程采用事件模式采集；构建端到端全自动数据处理流水线，生成标准科学级能谱产品；开放自2023年12月起全部观测数据（含PV阶段）。

Result: 成功完成HEL1OS在轨标定与配置固化；建成稳定运行的自动化数据处理与产品生成系统；实现科学数据公开共享；初步展示了仪器在太阳耀斑等事件中的时间分辨与能谱分析能力。

Conclusion: HEL1OS已全面投入科学运行，其可靠的数据处理链路与开放数据政策为太阳高能物理研究提供了高质量硬X射线观测资源。

Abstract: HEL1OS (High Energy L1 Orbiting X-ray Spectrometer) is the hard X-ray spectrometer on-board Aditya-L1 solar mission, monitoring the Sun continuously in the hard X-ray band of 8 keV to 150 keV from the vantage observation platform around the Sun-Earth Lagragian Point 1 (L1). HEL1OS was commissioned in end of October, 2023 and the final instrument configuration was set by end of June, 2024 after all the performance verifications. The instrument always operates in the event mode, and the down-linked data is processed in an automated pipeline to generate Science-ready products comprising mainly of solar spectra in 8 keV to 150 keV energy band. The data products of observations since December, 2023 are hosted on the internet with open access. This includes data from the performance verification phase of the payload operations. In this paper, the details of the commissioning and performance verification phases, all the stages of the fully automated data pipeline, and the data products of HEL1OS are discussed. Also a few scientific results are discussed to illustrate the timing and spectral capabilities of the instrument in monitoring Sun in the X-ray energy bands.

</details>


### [140] [Project management of the Canadian Hydrogen Observatory and Radio-transient Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01452)
*Dallas Wulf,Matt Dobbs,Brian Hoff,Michael Rupen,Kendrick Smith,Keith Vanderlinde*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了加拿大氢观测与射电瞬变源探测器（CHORD）项目的管理框架，该框架帮助项目在固定预算和工期约束下，应对全球经济与技术波动带来的挑战，成功推进首期64碟天线子阵列建设并启动调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定预算和工期约束下，应对全球经济与技术波动带来的重大物流与管理挑战，确保CHORD这一大型射电干涉仪基础设施项目顺利推进。

Method: 采用系统化的项目管理框架，统筹协调建设进度、资源分配与风险应对，支持并行开展子阵列建设与早期调试工作。

Result: 成功完成首期64碟天线子阵列建设，进入仪器调试阶段，并为后续大规模阵列建设奠定基础。

Conclusion: 有效的项目管理框架是保障大型科研基础设施在复杂外部环境下按期、按预算交付的关键因素。

Abstract: The Canadian Hydrogen Observatory and Radio-transient Detector (CHORD) is a new drift-scan radio interferometer operating from 300-1500 MHz, optimized for 21cm intensity mapping, 21cm galaxy detection, pulsars, and transient detection and localization. The full instrument will feature a core array of 512 six-meter dishes, plus two 64-dish outriggers at several thousand kilometer baselines. The project is nearing completion of the first 64-dish sub array, which will enable commissioning activities to begin in parallel with the remaining construction. In this presentation, we discuss the project management framework that has enabled us to navigate the logistical challenges of a fixed-budget, fixed-timeline research infrastructure project through a period of significant global economic and technological volatility.

</details>
